2026-11-01T16:40:10.9674220Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 with P and rerun the byte-equal three-slot V candidate under a direction-only margin, on the round-17 image without a build

P's configuration has scored 10002.75 to 10020.58 over five runs. Round
17 (41287043170) gated P3, native V on three slots with V loaded before
the K acquire: its attention, scale and O were byte-equal to P, and its
pooled median at 3994+3994 was faster than P on all 12 GPUs (1.7-7.1 us,
median 5.4 us), but five GPUs stayed under the 5 us margin, so it was
refused after only its first shape.

The 5 us margin guards against adopting a numerically different
candidate on a noise-sized gain. P3 cannot change numerics, so its
margin only has to confirm the direction of the speed difference. The
rule for P3 (by Codex, first committed as 75cf199 on
perf/pp4-r21-p3adopt-20261102 and carried here unchanged so the push
starts serving) is now:
- attention, scale and O byte-equal to P on the original and two
  changed inputs, as before;
- for each of the seven pairs, pooled savings above zero on all 12
  GPUs and a median of at least 3 us across them;
- no more than 2 us slower in either the forward or the reverse median
  on any GPU and shape;
- FP32 reference, singles, the 1200-call endurance and the UUID-set
  aggregate unchanged. P's 5 us rule against control is unchanged.
Applied to round 17's fragments (kept as a test fixture) the new rule
still refuses P3: one GPU was 2.34 us slower in forward order on the
first shape. That cell is noise-sized and round 17 never measured the
other six shapes, so this run measures them all under the new rule; if
P3 fails again, P serves as before.

No kernel change, so this reuses the round-17 image, which carries both
P and P3: path, sha256 149e0fc9d1f71733a25ddba2939f3373db8e33740c29575e4bd33d0fcc82fa3f,
12298383360 bytes and provenance, container_build_run_id 41287043170,
container_sha256, container_size_bytes, container_source_commit 179c121,
PINNED_IMAGE and the test prefixes. fa3_v3_gate is true.

Validation: on this exact tree Codex ran the full suite both ways,
no-project CPython 3.12.12 and CI-style project CPython 3.13.12: 1468
passed, 90 skipped and 25 subtests passed in each.
